BUSINESS PERFORMANCE REVIEW                                                        業務表現回顧
Revenue                                                                            營業額
For the six months ended 30 June 2025 (the “Current Period”), the Group’s          截 至 2025 年 6 月 30 日 止 六 個 月（「本 期」），本 集
overall revenue amounted to RMB36,264 million, representing an increase of         團的整體營業額為人民幣 36,264 百萬元，較去年
RMB6,111 million or 20.3% compared with an overall revenue of RMB30,153            同期的整體營業額人民幣 30,153 百萬元，上升了
million for the Same Period of Previous Year.                                      人民幣 6,111 百萬元或 20.3%。

During the Current Period, the global economic environment remained volatile,      本期內，全球經濟環境仍然波動，持續的政策
with ongoing policy uncertainty and geopolitical turmoil further weighing on       不確定性與地緣政治動盪亦進一步令市場受壓。
the market. Under the gloom of the global tariff war, the shift in global supply   在全球關稅戰陰霾底下，環球供應鏈轉移及價
chains and price transmission had varying degrees of impact on                     格傳導對製造型企業乃至本集團各業務板塊產
manufacturing companies and various business sectors of the Group. The             生不同程度的影響；持續的房地產危機也進一
ongoing real estate crisis also further dampened overall market sentiment and      步打擊整體市場氣氛和消費信心。在宏觀經濟
consumer confidence. Amid an unclear macro-economic landscape, the Group           環境尚未明朗的情況下，本集團堅持推動產業
remained committed to driving industrial transformation and innovative             轉型和創新發展經營方針，同時緊貼市場最新
development strategies. We closely monitored the latest market trends and          發展動向，善用數字化、智能化及低碳化轉型
leveraged opportunities brought by digitisation, intelligence, and low-carbon      給產業帶來的各種契機，以堅實的技術基礎強
transformation to strengthen the research and development of our                   化自研技術研發和成果轉化，繼續為產業的高
proprietary technology, as well as the commercialisation of innovations based      質量發展注入新動能。本集團精準研判前瞻趨
on our solid technical foundation. This continues to inject new momentum into      勢，並且積極響應實現碳達峰與碳中和（「雙碳」）
the high-quality development of our industry. The Group has accurately             目標，已經從電視領域擴展到智能家電、智能
anticipated forward-looking trends and actively responded to the goal of           終端，再成功跨界進入新能源領域，走出了一
achieving carbon peak and carbon neutrality (the “Double Carbon”) targets. It      條「從智能家電到雙碳生態」的特色之路。
has expanded from the television sector into smart household appliances and
smart devices, and successfully ventured into the new energy sector,
embarking on a distinctive path “From Smart Appliances to Double Carbon
Ecology”.

During the Current Period, constrained by slower-than-expected economic            於本期，受限於低於預期的經濟復甦步伐，以
recovery and challenges such as weak consumer confidence, the Group faced          及市場面臨消費信心不足等問題，本集團承受
certain operational pressures. The net profit for the Current Period amounted      一 定 經 營 壓 力。本 期 淨 利 潤 為 人 民 幣 365 百 萬
to RMB365 million, representing a decrease of RMB349 million or 48.9%              元，較去年同期的人民幣 714 百萬元減少人民幣
compared to RMB714 million for the Same Period of Previous Year. Additionally,     349 百萬元或 48.9%。同時，本集團現代服務業業
the construction development business under the Group’s modern services            務板塊中建設發展業務受中國房地產市場持續
business sector was impacted by the prolonged downturn in China’s real             低迷影響而計提減值及導致該業務業績表現欠
estate market, leading to impairment provision and resulted in poor business       佳。儘管仍然複雜多變的營商環境正考驗著本
performance of this business sector. Although the complex and volatile             集團的韌性，本集團採取穩健及靈活的營運策
business environment continued to test the Group’s resilience, the Group has       略，包括優化資源配置及加快調整庫存結構去
adopted a prudent and flexible operating strategy. This included optimising        應對激烈競爭環境，並以優質的產品質量打造
resource allocation and accelerating inventory structure adjustments to            強而有力的品牌競爭力，經營狀況及財務表現
navigate the highly competitive environment, as well as building a robust          保持穩定，各項工作有序推進。
brand competitiveness with high-quality product quality and maintaining
stable operating conditions and financial performance, to advance all aspects
of its work in an orderly manner.

Gross Profit Margin                                                                 毛利率
For the six months ended 30 June 2025, the overall gross profit margin of the       截 至 2025 年 6 月 30 日 止 六 個 月，本 集 團 之 整 體
Group was 12.3%, representing a decrease of 1.5 percentage points in                毛利率為 12.3%，較去年同期的 13.8% 下跌 1.5 個百

1.   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                          1.    編製基礎
     The condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared                  簡明綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
     in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard (“HKAS”) 34 “Interim               頒佈之香港會計準則第 34 號中期財務報告
     Financial Reporting ” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public         以及香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規
     Accountants (the “HKICPA”) as well as with the applicable disclosure                則附錄 D2 之適用披露規定而編製。
     requirements of Appendix D2 to the Rules Governing the Listing of
     Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.

     The preparation of the condensed consolidated financial statements                  編製簡明綜合財務報表需要管理層作出估
     requires management to make estimates and assumptions that                          計及假設，而該等估計及假設可影響於報
     affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and, disclosure of            告期末所呈報之資產與負債金額及所披露
     contingent liabilities at the end of the reporting period and the reported          之或然負債，以及報告期內所呈報之收支
     amount of revenue and expenses during the reporting period.                         金額。

     The Group’s operations are seasonal. The revenue from September to                  本集團業務受季節因素影響，消費類電子
     January (the peak season for sales of consumer electronic products in               產品於中國大陸之銷售旺季為每年 9 月至
     the mainland China) is relatively higher than the revenue from the rest of          次年 1 月，此段期間之營業額相對高於其
     the year. Results for interim periods are not necessarily indicative of the         他月份之營業額，故中期業績未必反映整
     results for the entire financial year. This interim report should be read,          個財政年度之業績。此中期報告應與本集
     where relevant, in conjunction with the annual report of the Group for              團截至 2024 年 12 月 31 日止年度之年報（倘
     the year ended 31 December 2024.                                                    相關）一併閱讀。

4.   SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                          4.    分部資料
     The Group is organised into operating business units according to the              本集團乃根據貨品銷售或提供服務之性質
     nature of the goods sold or services provided. The Group determines its            分類出營運業務單位。因此，本集團根據
     operating segments based on these business units by reference to the               業務單位所銷售之貨品或所提供之服務決
     goods sold or services provided, for the purpose of reporting to the chief         定其營運分部，以便向主要經營決策者（即
     operating decision maker (“CODM”) (i.e. the executive directors of the             本公司之執行董事）呈報。提供獨立財務
     Company). Individual operating segments for which discrete financial               資料的個別經營分部由主要經營決策者鑒
     information is available are identified by the CODM and are operated               定並由其各自的管理團隊經營。該等個別
     by their respective management teams. These individual operating                   經營分部合計總額達致本集團可呈報分部。
     segments are aggregated in arriving at the reportable segments of the
     Group.

     Specifically, the Group’s reportable segments under HKFRS 8 Operating              具體而言，本集團於本中期期間根據香港
     Segments in the current interim period are as follows:                             財務報告準則第 8 號營運分部 確定之呈報
                                                                                        分部如下：

     1.      Smart Household Appliances             –    manufacture and sale of smart TV, smart white appliances and other smart
             Business                                    appliances such as smart air conditioners, smart refrigerators, smart washing
             智能家電業務                                      machines, smart kitchen appliances in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”)
                                                         and overseas markets, intelligent manufacturing, provision and sales of internet
                                                         value-added services of Coocaa system in the PRC, among others
                                                         中華人民共和國（「中國」）及海外市場之智能電視、智能白家電產品
                                                         及其他智能電器（例如智能空調、智能冰箱、智能洗衣機、智能廚房
                                                         電器等）之製造及銷售、中國之智能製造，以及提供及銷售酷開系統
                                                         的互聯網增值服務等

     2.      Smart Systems Technology               –    manufacture and sale of home access systems, intelligent manufacturing,
             Business                                    automotive electronic systems, lighting products, security system and other
             智能系統技術業務                                    electronic products
                                                         家庭接入系統、智能製造、汽車電子系統、照明產品、安防系統及其

     Each of the operating segments under smart household appliances,                   智能家電、智能系統技術及新能源業務之
     smart systems technology and new energy businesses include                         各營運分部包括各自業務下之製造及╱或

     the respective businesses. Each of these operations is considered as a             策 者 視 為 獨 立 經 營 分 部。就 分 部 報 告 而
     separate operating segment by the CODM. For the purpose of segment                 言，此等個別經營分部已結集為可呈報分

     into reportable segments as set out above in order to present a more               本公司董事認為，提供各經營分部之詳情
     systematic and structured segment information. To give details of                  將導致篇幅過於冗長。

     segment. No analysis of segment asset and segment liability is                         出決定。並無呈報分部資產及分部負債分
     presented as the CODM does not review such information for the                         析，因為主要經營決策者不就資源分配及
     purposes of resources allocation and performance assessment.                           表現評估審閱有關資料。因此，僅呈列分
     Therefore, only segment revenue and segment results are presented.                     部收入及分部業績。

16. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL                                          16. 金融工具之公允值計量
    INSTRUMENTS
    (a)   Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and financial                  (a)   按經常性基準以公允值計量本
          liabilities that are measured at fair value on a                                集團的金融資產及金融負債的
          recurring basis                                                                 公允值
          Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are              本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於
          measured at fair value at the end of each reporting period. The                 每 個 報 告 期 末 均 以 公 允 值 計 量。以
          following table gives information about how the fair values of                  下表格提供如何釐定該等金融資產
          these financial assets and financial liabilities are determined (in             及金融負債之公允值的資料（特別是
          particular, the valuation technique(s) and inputs used), as well                所採用的估值技術和輸入數據），以
          as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the fair value              及公允值層級中根據輸入數據可觀
          measurements are categorised (levels 1 to 3) based on the degree                察程度分類的公允值計量等級（級別
          to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.               1 至 3）。

          •     Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •    級別1公允值計量是來自活躍
                quoted prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical                      市場對同類資產或負債的報價
                assets or liabilities;                                                        （未經調整）；

          •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別2公允值計量是來自撇除
                inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1                       級別 1 內該資產或負債直接（等
                that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly               同 價 格）或 間 接（源 自 價 格）可
                (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices); and                觀察報價外之輸入數據；及

          •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別3公允值計量是來自估值
                valuation techniques that include inputs for the asset or                     技術，包含並非基於可觀察市
                liability that are not based on observable market data                        場數據的資產或負債之輸入數
                (unobservable inputs).                                                        據（不可觀察輸入數據）。

INTRODUCTION                                                                        引言
We have reviewed the condensed consolidated financial statements of                 本核數師已審閱第 29 至 67 頁所載的簡明綜合財
Skyworth Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively           務報表，包括創維集團有限公司（「貴公司」）及
referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 29 to 67, which comprise the           其 附 屬 公 司（統 稱「貴 集 團」）於 2025 年 6 月 30 日
condensed consolidated statement of financial position as of 30 June 2025           的簡明綜合財務狀況表，以及截至該日止六個
and the related condensed consolidated statement of profit or loss and              月期間的相關簡明綜合損益及其他全面收入表、
other comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and statement            權益變動表及現金流量表及若干附註解釋。根

are set out in note 20 of the condensed consolidated financial statements.                                註 20。該等交易並非上市規則所界定的關連交
These transactions were not connected transactions within the meaning of                                  易且主要於本集團日常業務過程中所訂立，條
the Listing Rules and were mainly entered into by the Group in the ordinary                               款乃按一般商業條款及按公平原則磋商後釐定。
course of business and the terms were on normal commercial terms and on                                   除上述關連人士之交易外，本集團亦與上市規
an arm’s length basis. Other than these related party transactions, the Group                             則所界定之本公司關連人士進行若干交易。該
also entered into certain transactions with connected persons of the Company                              等關連交易全部符合上市規則第 14A.76(1) 條所載
within the meaning of the Listing Rules. These connected transactions all fall                            之最低豁免規定，因此獲全面豁免遵守申報、
under the de minimis provision set forth in Rule 14A.76(1) of the Listing Rules                           公告及獨立股東批准規定。本集團已根據其企
and are therefore fully exempted from the reporting, announcement and                                     業管治常規就關連交易進行定期檢討。
independent shareholders’ approval requirements. The Group has performed
regular review on the connected transactions in accordance with its corporate
governance practice.

Environmental Protection                                                           環境保護
The Company recognised environmental protection as an important part               本公司深明保護環境是體現企業社會責任重要
of corporate social responsibility. Therefore, the Group has implemented           的一環，因此本集團與各商業夥伴合力推行各
various green measures in cooperation with its business partners for energy        種節能減排的綠色措施，減少對環境的不良影
conservation and emission reduction, with an aim to mitigate the adverse           響。我們積極宣揚環境保護意識，同時指導不
impact on the environment. By actively promoting public awareness of               同業務板塊，希望將本集團的環保策略融入營
environmental protection and providing guidelines to different business            運細節當中，真正實現「綠色創維、綠色視聽、
segments, we intend to integrate the Group’s environmental protection              綠化世界」的願景。
strategies into every aspect of our operation, and finally realise its vision of
attaining “Green Skyworth, Green Audio-visual, Green World”.

The Company has adopted, in a comprehensive manner, the internationally             本公司已全面採用受國際認可的環境管理體系
recognised Environmental Management System (ISO 14001:2015) and Energy             （ ISO 14001：2015 ）和 能 源 管 理 體 系（ ISO 50001：
Management System (ISO 50001:2011) as the guiding principles of the Group in        2011）作為本集團在環境保護方面的指導原則。
environmental protection. The Group’s environmental policy comprises four           本集團以四個策略為主要環境綱領，分別為「生
main strategies, namely “Production Design”, “Operational Energy Saving”,           產設計」、「營運節能」、「環保概念」及「潔淨能
“Concepts of Environmental Protection” and “Clean Energy”.                          源」策略。

The “Production Design” strategy aims to optimise the overall production           「生產設計」策略旨在簡化整體生產系統，如優
system such as streamlining the production procedure, increasing the                化生產流程、提高模具利用率，以及考慮使用
mould utilisation rate and considering the use of recycled materials to             回收材料以減少資源消耗。在「營運節能」策略
minimise resources consumption. With the “Operational Energy Saving”                中，本集團將努力不懈推廣環保意識文化，深
strategy, the Group will relentlessly promote the environmental awareness           化員工隊伍的環保價值觀。本公司現正推動電
culture and embed the conservation value to its workforce. The Company is           子化處理文件，減少浪費紙張；我們亦於建築
promoting E-process of documents to reduce paper wastage; we are also               物內應用自然採光理念、可調節空調及建立完
applying natural lighting concepts and adjustable air-conditioning as well          善的廢物管理制度。「環保概念」策略的目標是
as establishing a robust waste management system in our buildings. The              將綠色措施落實到本集團從採購至交付終端產
“Concepts of Environmental Protection” strategy targets to implant the green        品各個流程的供應鏈中。這需要從本集團的產
initiatives into the Group’s supply chain from procurement to delivery of end       品設計、環保包裝，以至運輸排放等方面改善

The internal control system is designed to provide reasonable, but not            內部監控系統旨在提供合理（而非絕對）的保證，
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss, and manage,            以防出現嚴重誤報或損失的情況，並管理（而非
rather than eliminate, risks of failure in operational systems, and to ensure     完全杜絕）營運系統故障的風險，以及確保達致
achievement of the Group’s objectives.                                            本集團的目標。
